                                                                                                             Stock draw
                                                                                                                 The increase in throughput outpaced the rise in supply,

                                                                                                             leading to an apparent stock draw of about 531,000 b/d in
                                                                                                             September, Platts calculations based on throughput, output and
                                                                                                             import data showed.

                                                                                                                 The higher daily throughput came as China's domestic crude
                                                                                                             output also hit a six-month high of 4.45 million b/d (18.21 million
                                                                                                             mt) in September, NBS data showed, while imports rose to a
                                                                                                             five-month high of 9.06 million b/d, according to customs data
                                                                                                             released Oct. 14.

Gulf Coast jet differential gains 15.70 cents

Buckeye Pipeline turns positive after 10 days

European refineries underproduce jet fuel

    Platts on Oct. 19 assessed the benchmark Gulf Coast Jet
differential 15.70 cents/gallon stronger to an 11.50 cents/gal
discount to NYMEX December ULSD futures, while the outright
price rose 15.98 cents/gal to $4.611/gal.

    Similar gains were observed on the Atlantic Coast, where the
benchmark Buckeye Pipeline differential rose 12 cents/gal to an
8-cent/gal premium to NYMEX November ULSD futures, entering
positive territory for the first time in 10 days.

Subscriber Notes                                                                     Platts amends Abu Dhabi crude OSPs publication
                                                                                         Platts, part of S&P Global Energy, has updated its publication
Platts to amend freight calculation for Oman, Murban CFR
North Asia crude assessments Nov. 2                                                  of Abu Dhabi crude official selling prices effective Oct. 8, 2026,
                                                                                     in line with changes announced by Abu Dhabi National Oil Co.
    Platts, part of S&P Global Energy, will amend the freight                        (ADNOC).
calculation used in its daily Oman and Murban CFR North Asia
crude assessments, effective Nov. 2, 2026.                                               In July, ADNOC said it would adopt a prompt-month pricing
                                                                                     methodology based on the Platts Dubai benchmark (PCAAT00)
    Under the change, Platts will use the daily VLCC Gulf of Oman-                   plus an ADNOC-announced differential for the OSPs of its Abu
to-Far East freight assessment in $/mt (DOOEW00), which was                          Dhabi onshore and offshore crude grades, with effect from
launched Aug. 3, in its calculation of the outright price of Oman                    November 2026 loadings. ADNOC added that it would announce
and Murban delivered into North Asia at 4:30 pm Singapore time                       the OSP differentials for Murban, Das, Umm Lulu and Upper
(0830 GMT).                                                                          Zakum in the month prior to the delivery month.

    The change will better reflect the shipping economics for the                        Accordingly, Platts will publish the monthly OSP differentials
two grades, with Murban primarily loading from Fujairah port and                     for the four grades, basis Platts Dubai, beginning with the
Oman from Mina Al Fahal port outside the Strait of Hormuz.                           November differentials issued by ADNOC on Oct. 8, 2026.

3-DAY TRAINING
